(indices where index % 5 == 1)\n    etc.\n    \"\"\"\n    folds = []\n    for fold_idx in range(n_folds):\n        test_indices = np.arange(fold_idx, n_samples, n_folds)\n        train_indices = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n_samples), test_indices)\n        folds.append((train_indices, test_indices))\n    return folds\n\n# Actually, re-reading: \"fold k is the k-th consecutive slice\"\n# This means: divide into 5 consecutive chunks\n# fold 0: rows 0-27\n# fold 1: rows 28-55\n# fold 2: rows 56-83\n# fold 3: rows 84-111\n# fold 4: rows 112-139\n\ndef get_contiguous_folds_consecutive(n_samples, n_folds=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create 5 contiguous folds as consecutive slices.\n    \"\"\"\n    folds = []\n    fold_size = n_samples // n_folds\n    for fold_idx in range(n_folds):\n        test_start = fold_idx * fold_size\n        if fold_idx == n_folds - 1:\n            test_end = n_samples\n        else:\n            test_end = (fold_idx + 1) * fold_size\n        \n        test_indices = np.arange(test_start, test_end)\n        train_indices = np.concatenate([\n            np.arange(0, test_start),\n            np.arange(test_end, n_samples)\n        ])\n        folds.append((train_indices, test_indices))\n    return folds\n\n# Test\nfolds = get_contiguous_folds_consecutive(len(X), n_folds=5)\nfor i, (train, test) in enumerate(folds):\n    print(f\"Fold {i}: train size={len(train)}, test size={len(test)}, test indices={test[:5]}...{test[-5:]}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

1) * (w_max - w_min) + w_min\n    \n    return w_quant\n\n# Test compression\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nbits_options = [8, 4]\n\nresults_compression = {}\n\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for bits in bits_options:\n        # Apply sparsity then quantization\n        w_compressed = apply_sparsity(dense_model.w, sparsity)\n        w_compressed = apply_quantization(w_compressed, bits)\n        \n        # Create model with compressed weights\n        model_compressed = LogisticRegression(lambda_reg=0.1)\n        model_compressed.w = w_compressed\n        model_compressed.b = dense_model.b\n        \n        # Evaluate on full data\n        probs_compressed = model_compressed.predict_proba(X)\n        brier_compressed = brier_score(y, probs_compressed)\n        \n        # Retention\n        retention = dense_brier / brier_compressed\n        \n        config_name = f\"sparsity{sparsity}_bits{bits}\"\n        results_compression[config_name] = {\n            'sparsity': sparsity,\n            'bits': bits,\n            'brier': brier_compressed,\n            'retention': retention,\n            'num_zeros': np.sum(w_compressed == 0)\n        }\n        \n        print(f\"{config_name}: dense_brier={dense_brier:.6f}, compressed_brier={brier_compressed:.6f}, retention={retention:.6f}, zeros={np.sum(w_compressed == 0)}\")\n\n# Find best config\nbest_config_name = max(results_compression.keys(), key=lambda k: results_compression[k]['retention'])\nprint(f\"\\nBest config: {best_config_name} with retention {results_compression[best_config_name]['retention']:.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

1) * (w_max - w_min) + w_min\n    \n    return w_quant\n\n# Test compression\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nbits_options = [8, 4]\n\nresults_compression = {}\n\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for bits in bits_options:\n        # Apply sparsity then quantization\n        w_compressed = apply_sparsity(dense_model.w, sparsity)\n        w_compressed = apply_quantization(w_compressed, bits)\n        \n        # Create model with compressed weights\n        model_compressed = LogisticRegression(lambda_reg=0.1)\n        model_compressed.w = w_compressed\n        model_compressed.b = dense_model.b\n        \n        # Evaluate on full data\n        probs_compressed = model_compressed.predict_proba(X)\n        brier_compressed = brier_score(y, probs_compressed)\n        \n        # Retention\n        retention = dense_brier / brier_compressed\n        \n        config_name = f\"sparsity{sparsity}_bits{bits}\"\n        results_compression[config_name] = {\n            'sparsity': sparsity,\n            'bits': bits,\n            'brier': brier_compressed,\n            'retention': retention,\n            'num_zeros': np.sum(w_compressed == 0)\n        }\n        \n        print(f\"{config_name}: dense_brier={dense_brier:.6f}, compressed_brier={brier_compressed:.6f}, retention={retention:.6f}, zeros={np.sum(w_compressed == 0)}\")\n\n# Find best config\nbest_config_name = max(results_compression.keys(), key=lambda k: results_compression[k]['retention'])\nprint(f\"\\nBest config: {best_config_name} with retention {results_compression[best_config_name]['retention']:.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"sparsity40_bits8: dense_brier=0.141762, compressed_brier=0.144027, retention=0.984274, zeros=16"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

y_true) ** 2)\n\nclass LogisticRegression:\n    def __init__(self, lambda_reg=0.0):\n        self.lambda_reg = lambda_reg\n        self.w = None\n        self.b = None\n    \n    def fit(self, X, y, n_iters=200, lr=0.1):\n        \"\"\"\n        Fit using full-batch gradient descent\n        w -= lr * (grad_w / n + lambda * w)\n        b -= lr * (grad_b / n)\n        \"\"\"\n        n_samples, n_features = X.shape\n        self.w = np.zeros(n_features)\n        self.b = 0.0\n        \n        for _ in range(n_iters):\n            # Forward pass with clipping\n            logits = np.clip(X @ self.w + self.b, -30, 30)\n            probs = sigmoid(logits)\n            \n            # Compute gradients\n            errors = probs - y\n            grad_w = X.T @ errors / n_samples\n            grad_b = np.sum(errors) / n_samples\n            \n            # Update weights with L2 regularization\n            self.w -= lr * (grad_w + self.lambda_reg * self.w)\n            self.b -= lr * grad_b\n        \n        return self\n    \n    def predict_proba(self, X):\n        \"\"\"Predict probability\"\"\"\n        logits = np.clip(X @ self.w + self.b, -30, 30)\n        return sigmoid(logits)\n\n# Test on simple case\nmodel = LogisticRegression(lambda_reg=0.0)\nmodel.fit(X, y, n_iters=200, lr=0.1)\nprobs = model.predict_proba(X)\nscore = brier_score(y, probs)\nprint(f\"Full data Brier score with lambda=0: {score:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Predictions sample: {probs[:5]}\")\n"}}],"api":"anthropic-messages","provider":"anthropic","model":"claude-haiku-4-5","usage":{"input":4013,"output":743,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"totalTokens":4756,"cost":{"input":0.004013,"output":0.0037150000000000004,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.0077280000000000005}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1788438315068,"responseId":"msg_011CegW4eevQNNLKTWAxnGKJ"},{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"toolu_0179ahnysPEiyGDr8CxMh8zF","toolName":"ipython","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Full data Brier score with lambda=0: 0.122518\nPredictions sample: [0.2495508  0.96241292 0.21193175 0.83208108 0.93269538]\n"}],"details":{"durationMs":14,"status":"ok","stdout":"Full data Brier score with lambda=0: 0.122518\nPredictions sample: [0.2495508  0.96241292 0.21193175 0.83208108 0.93269538]\n","stderr":"","kernelRestarted":false},"isError":false,"timestamp":1788438320728},{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Good, the model is working. min and max.\n    \"\"\"\n    w_quant = weights.copy()\n    \n    # Get non-zero weights\n    non_zero_mask = w_quant != 0\n    if not np.any(non_zero_mask):\n        return w_quant\n    \n    non_zero_w = w_quant[non_zero_mask]\n    w_min = np.min(non_zero_w)\n    w_max = np.max(non_zero_w)\n    \n    if w_min == w_max:\n        # All non-zero weights are the same\n        return w_quant\n    \n    # Map to [0, 2^bits - 1]\n    n_levels = 2 ** bits\n    quantized = np.round((non_zero_w - w_min) / (w_max - w_min) * (n_levels - 1))\n    \n    # Map back to original range\n    w_quant[non_zero_mask] = quantized / (n_levels - 1) * (w_max - w_min) + w_min\n    \n    return w_quant\n\n# Test compression\nsparsities = [20, 40, 60]\nbits_options = [8, 4]\n\nresults_compression = {}\n\nfor sparsity in sparsities:\n    for bits in bits_options:\n        # Apply sparsity then quantization\n        w_compressed = apply_sparsity(dense_model.w, sparsity)\n        w_compressed = apply_quantization(w_compressed, bits)\n        \n        # Create model with compressed weights\n        model_compressed = LogisticRegression(lambda_reg=0.1)\n        model_compressed.w = w_compressed\n        model_compressed.b = dense_model.b\n        \n        # Evaluate on full data\n        probs_compressed = model_compressed.predict_proba(X)\n        brier_compressed = brier_score(y, probs_compressed)\n        \n        # Retention\n        retention = dense_brier / brier_compressed\n        \n        config_name = f\"sparsity{sparsity}_bits{bits}\"\n        results_compression[config_name] = {\n            'sparsity': sparsity,\n            'bits': bits,\n            'brier': brier_compressed,\n            'retention': retention,\n            'num_zeros': np.sum(w_compressed == 0)\n        }\n        \n        print(f\"{config_name}: dense_brier={dense_brier:.6f}, compressed_brier={brier_compressed:.6f}, retention={retention:.6f},
